To address the MDVRP's computational complexity, neural-based combinatorial optimization methods offer a promising scalable alternative to traditional approaches. However, neural-based methods typically rely on rigid architectures and input encodings tailored to specific problem formulations. In real-world settings, heterogeneous constraints create multiple MDVRP variants, limiting the applicability of such models. While multi-task learning (MTL) has begun to accelerate the development of unified neural-based solvers, prior works focus almost exclusively on single-depot VRPs, leaving the MDVRP unaddressed. To bridge this gap, we propose Feature-wise Linear Modulation for Cross-Problem Multi-Depot Vehicle Routing (FiLMMeD), a novel unified neural-based model for 24 different MDVRP variants. We introduce three main contributions: (1) to improve the model's generalization, we augment the standard Transformer encoder with Feature-wise Linear Modulation (FiLM), which dynamically conditions learned internal representations based on the active set of constraints; (2) we provide an initial demonstration of Preference Optimization in the MTL setting, establishing it as a superior alternative to Reinforcement Learning for future MTL works; (3) to mitigate the generalization gap caused by the introduction of multi-depot constraints, we introduce a targeted curriculum learning strategy that progressively exposes the model to increasingly more complex constraint interactions. Extensive experiments on 24 MDVRP variants (including 8 novel formulations) and 16 single-depot VRPs confirm the effectiveness of FiLMMeD, which consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines. Our code is available at: https://github.com/AJ-Correa/FiLMMeD/tree/main

</details>


### [108] [Auto-FlexSwitch: Efficient Dynamic Model Merging via Learnable Task Vector Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.28109)
*Junqi Gao,Dazhi Zhang,Zhichang Guo,Biqing Qi,Yi Ran,Wangmeng Zuo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出T-Switch系列方法解决动态模型合并中的存储开销问题，通过分析任务向量的类脉冲激活特性和低比特表示鲁棒性，设计了从分解压缩到自适应学习的完整解决方案，最终实现高效且保真度高的任务向量存储与动态合并。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动态合并方法虽然通过灵活组合任务特定参数维持了高性能，但需要为每个任务存储独立参数，导致存储开销过大，无法满足实际部署需求。

Method: T-Switch将任务向量分解为二元稀疏掩码、符号向量和标量缩放因子三个紧凑组件；Auto-Switch通过特征相似度检索自动组合任务向量；FlexSwitch采用可学习门控稀疏化(LGS)和位宽自适应选择(BAS)联合优化压缩策略，并使用稀疏感知存储策略(SASS)选择编码结构；Auto-FlexSwitch结合KNN推理和可学习低秩度量实现高效推理。

Result: 实验证明微调权重增量（任务向量）具有类脉冲激活模式和高鲁棒性，能够在高位压缩率下保持高保真近似，且FlexSwitch能将静态压缩规则转化为自适应学习，Auto-FlexSwitch支持高效任务向量压缩。

Conclusion: T-Switch通过三组件分解实现了高保真压缩，Auto-Switch实现了免训练的自动组装，FlexSwitch实现了自适应的压缩策略学习，Auto-FlexSwitch整合以上技术，提供了高效的任务向量压缩动态模型合并方案，有效平衡了存储开销与性能。

Abstract: Model merging has attracted attention as an effective path toward multi-task adaptation by integrating knowledge from multiple task-specific models. Among existing approaches, dynamic merging mitigates performance degradation caused by conflicting parameter updates across tasks by flexibly combining task-specific parameters at inference time, thereby maintaining high performance. However, these methods require storing independent parameters for each task, resulting in prohibitive storage overhead. To address this issue, we first experimentally demonstrate that the fine-tuned weight increments (referred to as task vectors) exhibit an impulse-like activation pattern and high robustness to low-bit representations. Driven by this insight, we propose T-Switch, which decomposes task vectors into three compact components: a binary sparse mask, a sign vector, and a scalar scaling factor, achieving high-fidelity approximation at high compression ratios. We then introduce Auto-Switch, a training-free merging scheme that automatically composes task vectors via feature similarity retrieval. Building on this, we develop Auto-Switch, a training-free merging scheme that automatically assembles task vectors through feature similarity retrieval. Furthermore, to transform task vector sparsification and quantization from static rules to adaptive learning, we propose FlexSwitch, a learnable framework which jointly optimizes the compression strategy for each model unit via Learnable Gating Sparsification (LGS) and Bit-width Adaptive Selection (BAS), while employing the Sparsity-Aware Storage Strategy (SASS) to select the optimal storage encoding structure. Finally, by incorporating a K-Nearest Neighbor (KNN) inference scheme with a learnable low-rank metric, we present Auto-FlexSwitch, a dynamic model merging approach that supports highly efficient task vector compression.

</details>


### [109] [Do Sparse Autoencoders Capture Concept Manifolds?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.28119)
*Usha Bhalla,Thomas Fel,Can Rager,Sheridan Feucht,Tal Haklay,Daniel Wurgaft,Siddharth Boppana,Matthew Kowal,Vasudev Shyam,Jack Merullo,Atticus Geiger,Ekdeep Singh Lubana*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) are widely used to extract interpretable features from neural network representations, often under the implicit assumption that concepts correspond to independent linear directions. However, a growing body of evidence suggests that many concepts are instead organized along low-dimensional manifolds encoding continuous geometric relationships. This raises three basic questions: what does it mean for an SAE to capture a manifold, when do existing SAE architectures do so, and how? We develop a theoretical framework that answers these questions and show that SAEs can capture manifolds in two fundamentally different ways: globally, by allocating a compact group of atoms whose linear span contains the entire manifold, or locally, by distributing it across features that each selectively tile a restricted region of the underlying geometry. Empirically, we find that SAEs suboptimally recover continuous structures, mixing the global subspace and local tiling solutions in a fragmented regime we call dilution. This explains why manifold structure is rarely visible at the level of individual concepts and motivates post-hoc unsupervised discovery methods that search for coherent groups of atoms rather than isolated directions. More broadly, our results suggest that future representation learning methods should treat geometric objects, not just individual directions, as the basic units of interpretability.

</details>


### [110] [Global Optimality for Constrained Exploration via Penalty Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.28144)
*Florian Wolf,Ilyas Fatkhullin,Niao He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出策略梯度惩罚（PGP）方法解决约束最大熵探索问题，通过二次罚函数正则化和伪奖励构造，在策略参数化诱导的非凸条件下，利用隐藏凸性和强对偶性建立了全局收敛保证，实现可部署的近优近似可行策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 约束最大熵探索面临两大挑战：（1）熵最大化缺乏加性结构，传统Bellman方程方法不可用；（2）可扩展方法需要策略参数化，导致目标和约束非凸。现有的无模型策略梯度方法（如Ying et al., 2025）只能保证弱遗憾和遍历平均，无法输出单一可部署的近优近似可行策略。因此需要新的理论框架和方法。

Method: 提出策略梯度惩罚（PGP）方法：一种单环策略空间方法，通过二次罚函数正则化强制执行一般凸占有度量约束。核心思想是构造伪奖励以获得惩罚目标的梯度估计，进而利用经典策略梯度定理。关键贡献包括：建立惩罚目标的正则性（光滑性），利用隐藏凸性和强对偶性证明全局收敛性。

Result: 理论上建立了惩罚目标的正则性和全局最后迭代收敛保证，达到ε-最优约束熵值且约束违背有界（O(ε)）。实验上通过网格世界基准测试的消融实验验证了各组件的有效性，并在两个具有挑战性的连续控制任务上展示了可扩展性。

Conclusion: PGP方法通过二次罚函数正则化处理一般凸占有度量约束，利用隐藏凸性和强对偶性，在策略诱导的非凸性下实现全局收敛。该工作克服了现有方法的局限性，为约束最大熵探索提供了首个可部署的近优、近似可行策略的理论保证，并在连续控制任务上验证了可扩展性。

Abstract: Efficient exploration is a central problem in reinforcement learning and is often formalized as maximizing the entropy of the state-action occupancy measure. While unconstrained maximum-entropy exploration is relatively well understood, real-world exploration is often constrained by safety, resource, or imitation requirements. This constrained setting is particularly challenging because entropy maximization lacks additive structure, rendering Bellman-equation-based methods inapplicable. Moreover, scalable approaches require policy parameterization, inducing non-convexity in both the objective and the constraints. To our knowledge, the only prior model-free policy-gradient approach for this setting under general policy parameterization is due to Ying et al. (2025). Unfortunately, their guarantees are limited to weak regret and ergodic averages, which do not imply that the final output is a single deployable policy that is near-optimal and nearly feasible. In this work we take a different approach to this problem, and propose Policy Gradient Penalty (PGP) method, a single-loop policy-space method that enforces general convex occupancy-measure constraints via quadratic-penalty regularization. PGP constructs pseudo-rewards that yield gradient estimates of the penalized objective, subsequently exploiting the classical Policy Gradient Theorem. We further establish the regularity of the penalized objective, providing the smoothness properties needed to justify the convergence of PGP. Leveraging hidden convexity and strong duality, we then establish global last-iterate convergence guarantees, attaining an $ε$-optimal constrained entropy value with $ε$ bounded constraint violation despite policy-induced non-convexity. We validate PGP through ablations on a grid-world benchmark and further demonstrate scalability on two challenging continuous-control tasks.

</details>


### [111] [Explainable Load Forecasting with Covariate-Informed Time Series Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.28149)
*Matthias Hertel,Alexandra Nikoltchovska,Sebastian Pütz,Ralf Mikut,Benjamin Schäfer,Veit Hagenmeyer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对时间序列基础模型（TSFMs）提出了一种高效的SHAP解释算法，利用模型对输入上下文和协变量的灵活性实现可扩展的时间掩码和协变量掩码，验证了Chronos-2和TabPFN-TS在日负荷预测任务上可达到与专用模型相当的性能，同时提供可解释的预测结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列基础模型（TSFMs）作为通用预测模型在能源系统中具有应用潜力，但在电网等关键基础设施中需要透明性和可解释性以确保信任和可靠性，纯黑盒模型无法满足这一要求，因此需要增强TSFMs的可解释性。

Method: 利用TSFMs对输入上下文长度和协变量的灵活性，通过高效的时间掩码和协变量掩码策略选择性遗漏输入，结合Shapley加性解释（SHAP）方法实现可扩展的模型预测解释。实验评估了Chronos-2和TabPFN-TS两种TSFMs在输电系统运营商日负荷预测任务上的表现。

Result: Chronos-2和TabPFN-TS在零样本设置下的预测性能与专门训练的Transformer模型相当，SHAP解释结果与领域知识相符，模型能够合理利用天气和日历信息进行负荷预测。

Conclusion: 研究证明了TSFMs可以作为一种透明且可靠的运营能源预测工具，在零样本设置下即可达到与专门训练的Transformer模型相当的预测性能，且SHAP解释与领域知识一致，验证了模型对天气和日历信息的合理利用。

Abstract: Time Series Foundation Models (TSFMs) have recently emerged as general-purpose forecasting models and show considerable potential for applications in energy systems. However, applications in critical infrastructure like power grids require transparency to ensure trust and reliability and cannot rely on pure black-box models. To enhance the transparency of TSFMs, we propose an efficient algorithm for computing Shapley Additive Explanations (SHAP) tailored to these models. The proposed approach leverages the flexibility of TSFMs with respect to input context length and provided covariates. This property enables efficient temporal and covariate masking (selectively withholding inputs), allowing for a scalable explanation of model predictions using SHAP. We evaluate two TSFMs - Chronos-2 and TabPFN-TS - on a day-ahead load forecasting task for a transmission system operator (TSO). In a zero-shot setting, both models achieve predictive performance competitive with a Transformer model trained specifically on multiple years of TSO data. The explanations obtained through our proposed approach align with established domain knowledge, particularly as the TSFMs appropriately use weather and calendar information for load prediction. Overall, we demonstrate that TSFMs can serve as transparent and reliable tools for operational energy forecasting.

</details>


### [112] [Strait: Perceiving Priority and Interference in ML Inference Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.28175)
*Haidong Zhao,Nikolaos Georgantas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Strait是一个面向高GPU利用率场景的ML推理服务系统，通过建模数据传输竞争和内核执行干扰，构建自适应延迟预测模型，并结合优先级感知调度，在保障高优先级任务实时性的同时维持系统整体公平性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ML推理服务系统在应对双优先级推理流量时存在两个关键缺陷：任务优先级支持不足，以及并发执行下延迟估计不准确，限制了其在关键任务场景的应用。

Method: Strait采用两层策略：一是建模数据传输竞争并通过自适应预测模型量化内核执行干扰；二是基于预测结果进行优先级感知的调度决策，实现差异化处理。

Result: 在高负载工作流测试中，Strait使高优先级任务的截止时间违规率降低1.02至11.18个百分点，同时低优先级任务仅承受可接受的性能开销。相比软件定义抢占方案，Strait展现出更均衡的性能表现。

Conclusion: Strait通过优先级感知调度和精确的延迟预测，在高GPU利用率场景下有效提升高优先级任务的实时性保障，同时保持对低优先级任务的公平性，性能优于现有软件抢占方案。

Abstract: Machine learning (ML) inference serving systems host deep neural network (DNN) models and schedule incoming inference requests across deployed GPUs. However, limited support for task prioritization and insufficient latency estimation under concurrent execution may restrict their applicability in on-premises scenarios. We present \emph{Strait}, a serving system designed to enhance deadline satisfaction for dual-priority inference traffic under high GPU utilization. To improve latency estimation, Strait models potential contention during data transfer and accounts for kernel execution interference through an adaptive prediction model. By drawing on these predictions, it performs priority-aware scheduling to deliver differentiated handling. Evaluation results under intense workloads suggest that Strait reduces deadline violations for high-priority tasks by 1.02 to 11.18 percentage points while incurring acceptable costs on low-priority tasks. Compared to software-defined preemption approaches, Strait also exhibits more equitable performance.

</details>


<div id='cs.DS'></div>

# cs.DS [[Back]](#toc)

### [113] [Online Monotone Metric Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27059)
*Christian Coester,Yichen Huang*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文提出在线单调度量嵌入新范式，通过允许距离单调递减来松弛传统约束，在静态场景打破Ω(log n log Δ)下界实现O(log² n)，动态场景下概率版本达到近乎最优的O(l log l)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典在线嵌入要求点到达时立即且不可撤销地嵌入，导致嵌入失真下界为Ω(min(n, log n log Δ))，限制了在线算法的性能上限。

Method: 提出在线单调度量嵌入（online monotone metric embeddings）概念，允许嵌入点间距离随时间单调递减；同时探索概率版本以支持动态场景（点可到达和离开）。

Result: 对于静态到达场景，单调嵌入实现O(log² n)失真，打破了原有的下界壁垒。对于动态场景，概率单调嵌入达到O(l log l)失真（l为同时存在的最大点数），逼近Ω(l)的下界。

Conclusion: 该研究通过引入单调性松弛条件，打破了传统在线度量嵌入的Ω(min(n, log n log Δ))下界限制，实现了O(log² n)的嵌入失真。动态场景下，概率单调嵌入达到O(l log l)的近乎最优结果。

Abstract: Metric embeddings into structured spaces, particularly hierarchically well-separated trees (HSTs), are a fundamental tool in the design of online algorithms. In the classical online embedding setting, points arrive sequentially and must be embedded irrevocably upon arrival, resulting in strong distortion lower bounds of $Ω(\min(n, \log n\log Δ))$, where $n$ is the number of points and $Δ$ their aspect ratio.
  We propose a novel relaxation, \emph{online monotone metric embeddings}, which allows distances between embedded points in the target space to decrease monotonically over time. Such relaxed embeddings remain compatible with many online algorithms. Moreover, this relaxation breaks existing lower bound barriers, enabling embeddings into HSTs with distortion $O(\log^2 n)$.
  We also study a dynamic variant, where points may both arrive and depart, seeking distortion guarantees in terms of the maximum number $l$ of simultaneously present points. For traditional embeddings, such bounds are impossible, and this limitation persists even for deterministic monotone embeddings. Surprisingly, probabilistic monotone embeddings allow for $O(l \log l)$ distortion, which is nearly optimal given an $Ω(l)$ lower bound.

</details>


### [114] [New Diameter Approximations via Distance Oracle Techniques](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27142)
*Yael Kirkpatrick,Liam Roditty,Richard Qi,Virginia Vassilevska Williams*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 该论文揭示了图直径近似算法与距离预言机研究之间此前未充分认识的深层联系，利用这一发现成功去随机化了此前最佳的直径近似算法CGR，获得了首个确定性直径近似权衡结果，并进一步将去随机化技术推广至更广泛的短路径近似领域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图直径计算既是算法研究的核心问题，也是细粒度复杂度理论中的基石性难题。尽管已有工作给出了有条件的下界，但已知的最优上界（包括随机近似方案）未能匹配该下界，且最佳直径近似方案缺乏已知的确定性等价版本。这一差距主要源于直径近似算法未能充分利用距离预言机研究中丰富多样的技术工具。

Method: 首先建立CGR直径近似算法与Thorup-Zwick的(2k-1)距离预言机之间的对应关系，通过对该对应关系的深入分析实现CGR算法的去随机化；随后系统地去随机化了距离预言机领域的其他核心技术，并将这些去随机化技术应用于直径近似问题及其他最短路径近似问题的研究中。

Result: 1. 证明了CGR直径近似算法与Thorup-Zwick (2k-1)距离预言机之间的深层联系；2. 实现了CGR算法的去随机化，获得了首个确定性直径近似权衡曲线；3. 去随机化了距离预言机中的多项核心技术，并将其应用于获得新的确定性直径近似算法；4. 成功将去随机化技术推广至多个最短路径近似领域的最优结果。

Conclusion: 成功建立了直径近似算法与距离预言机之间的深刻联系，突破了长期存在的"已知技术未被充分借鉴"的瓶颈。这一发现不仅填补了该领域的理论空白，也为后续研究提供了可复用的技术框架。

Abstract: Computing the diameter of a graph is a problem of great interest both in general algorithms research and specifically within fine-grained complexity, where it is a cornerstone hard problem. Recent work has achieved a full conditional lower bound tradeoff curve for both directed and undirected graphs. However, the best known upper bounds do not match the lower bounds. In particular, the best known approximation scheme for undirected graph diameter has not been improved. Moreover, this scheme is randomized and no similar deterministic scheme is known.
  Another fundamental field of research in shortest paths computation is the construction of approximate distance oracles. Thorup and Zwick [JACM'05] provided the first such distance oracle with constant query time and (conditionally) optimal space, and in the years since many advances have led to a vast toolbox of techniques and data structures.
  These two areas of research seem natural to combine since they both concern approximating shortest paths. However, the known diameter approximation algorithms only use a small subset of the techniques used in distance oracles research. In this work we show that in fact approximate diameter and distance oracles are intricately connected.
  We first demonstrate a strong connection between the current best known diameter approximation scheme of Cairo, Grossi and Rizzi ("CGR") and the $(2k-1)$-approximate distance oracle of Thorup and Zwick. This allows us to derandomize the CGR algorithm and obtain the first deterministic diameter approximation tradeoff.
  We further derandomize other central techniques in the field of distance oracles and use them to achieve new deterministic diameter approximation algorithms. Finally, we show how these new techniques can be used to derandomize many current best known results in various fields of shortest paths approximations.

</details>


### [115] [Designing sparse temporal graphs satisfying connectivity requirements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27227)
*Thomas Bellitto,Jules Bouton Popper,Justine Cauvi,Bruno Escoffier,Raphaëlle Maistre-Matus*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文研究了时间图中部分连通请求的边优化问题。有向情况下，最优边数为n减去连通分量数加上最小有向反馈顶点集大小，问题为NP完全但具有固定参数 tractability；无向情况下，强连通请求图可解的判定条件为非顶点分离的闭合路径必有公共顶点，且该条件可多项式时间检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已知完整连通需要至少2n-4条边，但实际应用中往往只需要部分顶点间的连通性，因此需要研究在满足部分连通需求时的最优边数问题。

Method: 有向情况：通过分析连通分量和最小有向反馈顶点集来推导最优边数下界与构造。无向情况：建立强连通请求图可解性的充分必要条件（闭合路径非顶点分离性），并设计多项式时间检验算法。

Result: 有向情况：最优时间边数为n-cc+dfvs；无向情况：强连通请求图可用n-1条边解当且仅当任意成对非顶点分离的闭合路径都有公共顶点。

Conclusion: 问题在有向情况下是NP完全的，但继承了有向反馈顶点集的固定参数可计算性质；对于无向情况，该条件可在多项式时间内检验。

Abstract: Connectivity of temporal graphs has been widely studied both as graph theory and as gossip theory. In particular, it is well known that in order to connect every vertex to every other, a temporal graph needs to have at least $2n-4$ edges where $n$ is the number of vertices. This paper investigates the optimal number of edges required to satisfy partial connectivity requirements. We introduce the problem of Connectivity Request Satisfaction where we are given a directed graph that we call the request graph, where an arc from $u$ to $v$ means that we need to be able to go from $u$ to $v$. Our goal is to build a temporal graph on the same vertex set with as few temporal edges as possible that would satisfy all the requests. When the graph we build is directed, we prove that the number of temporal arcs required is $n-\mathrm{cc}+\mathrm{dfvs}$ where $\mathrm{cc}$ is the number of connected component of the request graph and $\mathrm{dfvs}$ is the size of its smallest directed feedback vertex set. It follows that the problem is NP-complete but inherits fixed parameter tractability properties of Directed Feedback Vertex Set. When the graph we build is undirected, we establish a characterization of strongly connected request graphs that admit a solution with $n-1$ edges: it is possible if and only if any set of pairwise non-vertex-disjoint closed walks all share a common vertex. We prove that this criteria can be tested in polynomial time.

</details>


### [116] [A note on the parameter $\ell$ in Buchbinder--Feldman's deterministic submodular matroid algorithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27362)
*Shisheng Li*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文对Buchbinder-Feldman的$(1-1/e-\varepsilon)$近似算法进行了两个初等优化：通过Pólya-Szegő不等式改进$(1+1/\ell)^{-\ell}$的上界，以及利用交错级数尾部估计得到渐近精确的不等式。两种改进均仅缩小了查询复杂度的隐常数，渐近复杂度类别$\widetilde{O}_\varepsilon(nr)$保持不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Buchbinder和Feldman的原始算法使用参数$\ell = 1 + \lceil 1/\varepsilon \rceil$，其中对$(1+1/\ell)^{-\ell}$的界过于宽松，导致查询复杂度中的隐常数过大，存在进一步优化的空间。

Method: 1. 用经典Pólya--Szegő不等式$(1+1/\ell)^{-\ell} \le e^{-1}(1+1/(2\ell))$替代原文中松散的界；2. 采用交错级数尾部估计得到对数函数的精确不等式$(1+1/\ell)^{-\ell} \le e^{-1}\exp(1/(2\ell) - 1/(3\ell^2) + 1/(4\ell^3))$。所有不等式均在Lean 4和Mathlib中形式化验证。

Result: 第一种改进使$\ell = \lceil 1/(2e\varepsilon) \rceil$，隐常数缩小约$2^{0.816/\varepsilon}$倍；第二种改进得到渐近最优$\ell_\star = 1/(2e\varepsilon) - 5/12 + O(\varepsilon)$。

Conclusion: 两种改进均仅改善了复杂度中的隐常数，渐近复杂度类别$\widetilde{O}_\varepsilon(nr)$保持不变。这表明原算法框架已接近最优，改进空间主要在于技术性分析层面。

Abstract: Buchbinder and Feldman recently gave a deterministic $(1-1/e-\varepsilon)$-approximation for maximizing a non-negative monotone submodular function subject to a matroid constraint, with query complexity $\widetilde{O}_\varepsilon(nr)$. Their algorithm uses an integer parameter $\ell$, which Buchbinder and Feldman fix to $\ell = 1 + \lceil 1/\varepsilon \rceil$ via a loose bound on $(1+1/\ell)^{-\ell}$. We point out two purely elementary refinements. First, the classical Pólya--Szegő inequality $(1+1/\ell)^{-\ell} \le e^{-1}(1+1/(2\ell))$ replaces the loose step in their proof and permits $\ell = \lceil 1/(2e\varepsilon) \rceil$, shrinking the hidden constant in $\widetilde{O}_\varepsilon(nr)$ by a factor $\approx 2^{0.816/\varepsilon}$. Second, an alternating-series tail bound for $\log(1+t)$ yields the asymptotically sharp inequality $(1+1/\ell)^{-\ell} \le e^{-1}\exp(1/(2\ell) - 1/(3\ell^2) + 1/(4\ell^3))$, matching the true expansion of $(1+1/\ell)^{-\ell}$ through order $\ell^{-3}$ and translating into $\ell_\star = 1/(2e\varepsilon) - 5/12 + O(\varepsilon)$. The asymptotic class $\widetilde{O}_\varepsilon(nr)$ of the query complexity is unchanged in either case; only the implicit constant in $\varepsilon$ is improved. All inequalities in this note are formalized and machine-checked in Lean 4 against Mathlib.

</details>


### [117] [Computing the (k+2)-Edge-Connected Components in k-Edge-Connected Digraphs in Subquadratic Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27474)
*Loukas Georgiadis,Evangelos Kipouridis,Evangelos Kosinas,Charis Papadopoulos,Nikos Parotsidis*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文在有向图中计算k-边连通分量的研究取得突破性进展，首次打破经典 Nagamochi-Watanabe 的 O(mn) 时间界限，对于 k = o(n^{1/4}/√log n) 的情况实现了显著加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在无向图中，固定k的边连通分量可在线性时间内求解；但在有向图中，仅 k ≤ 3 时存在线性时间算法，对于任意固定 k > 3，自1993年 Nagamochi-Watanabe 的 O(mn) 时间算法以来一直未有改进，存在显著的理论空白。

Method: 引入关于有向边割的新结构洞察；简化和推广了 GKPP 框架（用于3-边连通分量）；结合新、旧技术手段；采用随机化算法设计。

Result: 提出随机化算法，在 O(k²m√n log n) 时间内计算 k-边连通有向图的 (k+2)-边连通分量；并给出专门算法，在 O(m√n log n) 时间内计算一般有向图的4-边连通分量。

Conclusion: 首次对任意常数 k > 3 突破 O(mn) 经典界限，是有向图边连通分量算法领域的重要里程碑，为后续研究开辟了新方向。

Abstract: Computing edge-connected components in directed and undirected graphs is a fundamental and well-studied problem in graph algorithms. In a very recent breakthrough, Korhonen [STOC 2025] showed that for any fixed $k$, the $k$-edge connected components of an undirected graph can be computed in linear time. In contrast, the directed case remains significantly more challenging: linear-time algorithms are only known for $k \le 3$, and for any fixed $k > 3$, the best known bound for sparse or moderately dense graphs is still the $O(mn)$-time algorithm of Nagamochi and Watanabe (1993).
  In this paper, we break the $O(mn)$ barrier for all $k = o(n^{1/4}/\sqrt{\log{n}})$. We present a randomized algorithm that computes the $(k+2)$-edge-connected components of a $k$-edge-connected directed graph in $O(k^2 m \sqrt{n} \log n)$ time, for any~$k$. This constitutes the first improvement over the classic Nagamochi--Watanabe bound for any constant $k > 3$. Our approach introduces new structural insights into directed edge-cuts and combines these with both new and existing techniques. A central contribution of our work is a substantial simplification and generalization of the framework introduced in~\cite{GKPP:3ECC}, which achieved an $\widetilde{O}(m\sqrt{m})$ bound for computing the $3$-edge-connected components of a digraph. In addition, we develop a variant of our algorithm that achieves the same $O(m \sqrt{n} \log n)$ running time for computing the $4$-edge-connected components of a \emph{general} directed graph.

</details>


### [118] [Smallest suffixient set maintenance in near-real-time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27548)
*Dominik Köppl,Gregory Kucherov*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: The size of the \textit{smallest suffixient set} of positions of a string recently emerged as a new measure of string \textit{repetitiveness} -- a measure reflecting how much of repetitive content the string contains. We study how to maintain the smallest suffixient set online in near-real-time, that is with small (in our case, polyloglog) worst-case time on processing each letter. Two frameworks are considered: when the text is given letter-by-letter in either a right-to-left or left-to-right direction. Our central algorithmic tool is Weiner's suffix tree algorithm and associated algorithmic primitives for its efficient implementation.

</details>


### [119] [An Exact 56-Addition, Rank-23 Scheme for General 3*3 Matrix Multiplication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27645)
*Yinqi Sun*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 该文提出了一个改进的23阶矩阵乘法算法，将加法/减法次数从58降至56，总标量运算为79次，适用于任意结合环，系数为三值，正确性由Brent方程验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 改善现有23阶方案中加法次数过多的问题（此前最好记录为58次加法），目标是减少总标量运算数。

Method: 通过构造23阶张量（对应系数三值化）并求解Brent方程实现。验证阶段展开为直线式程序并进行有限域和非交换实现测试。

Result: 实现了一个仅需56次加法/减法的23阶矩阵乘法算法，相比之前最优的58次加法方案减少了2次加法操作。

Conclusion: 提出了一个使用56次加法/减法和23次乘法的23阶矩阵乘法算法，共79次标量运算。该算法适用于任意结合环（包括非交换情形），系数为三值（-1,0,1）。正确性通过729个Brent方程验证，并在有限域和非交换环境下进行了额外测试。

Abstract: We present a rank-$23$ algorithm for general $3\times3$ matrix multiplication that uses $56$ additions/subtractions and $23$ multiplications, for a total of $79$ scalar operations in the standard bilinear straight-line model. This improves the recent sequence of $60$-, $59$-, and $58$-addition rank-$23$ schemes. The algorithm works over arbitrary associative, possibly noncommutative, coefficient rings. Its tensor coefficients are ternary, meaning that every coefficient lies in $\{-1,0,1\}$. Correctness is certified by the $729$ Brent equations over $\mathbb{Z}$, and the verifier also expands the straight-line program and performs additional finite-field and noncommutative implementation tests.

</details>


### [120] [Solving Hypergraph Laplacian Systems in Almost-Linear Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27651)
*Yuichi Yoshida*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文针对连通加权超图的割拉普拉斯算子泊松问题，提出随机近似线性时间求解算法。通过将Fenchel对偶重构为凸流问题并证明原恢复定理（每超边仅需一个标量），结合有限精度舍入与最小费用流求解，实现P^{1+o(1)}时间复杂度的原对偶恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对连通加权超图，需要高效的泊松问题求解器。基于割的超图拉普拉斯算子在自然输入规模P（超边尺寸总和）下的求解面临计算效率与原问题恢复的双重挑战。

Method: 1. 将Fenchel对偶问题重写为辅助O(P)弧图上的凸流问题，获得接近最优的对偶流；2. 提出恢复定理，证明在原恢复时不需要对对偶流在每个超边 gadget 中的详细路由，只需每个超边一个非负标量即可；3. 经过有限精度舍入后，这些标量定义辅助图上的线性代价最小费用流实例，通过精确求解恢复原始势；4. 从正则化目标到泊松求解器的顶点归约技术。

Result: 对于任意固定常数C>0，算法的随机运行时间为P^{1+o(1)}，高概率返回原始点和对偶证书，加性最优性间隙至多exp(-log^C P)。该方法可推广到同一切割型超图拉普拉斯算子的随机近似线性时间 resolvent/proximal 原语。

Conclusion: 该方法为基于割的超图拉普拉斯算子问题提供了高效的可求解原语，结合对偶流计算与新型恢复定理，能有效求解原始问题。算法具有良好的理论保证（高概率收敛）和接近线性的时间复杂度。

Abstract: For a connected weighted hypergraph, we give a randomized almost-linear-time solver for the Poisson problem for the cut-based hypergraph Laplacian in the natural input size $P=\sum_{e\in E}|e|$, the sum of hyperedge sizes. For every fixed constant $C>0$, our randomized algorithm runs in $P^{1+o(1)}$ time and, with high probability over its internal randomness, returns a primal point and a dual certificate, with additive optimality gap at most $\exp(-\log^C P)$.
  A key step is to rewrite the Fenchel dual as a convex-flow problem on an auxiliary $O(P)$-arc graph, yielding a near-optimal dual flow. The main difficulty is primal recovery, because this flow does not by itself determine a primal potential. Our main new ingredient is a recovery theorem showing that, for primal recovery, the detailed routing of the dual flow inside each hyperedge gadget can be discarded: one nonnegative scalar per hyperedge is enough. After the necessary finite-precision rounding, these scalars define a linear-cost min-cost-flow instance on the auxiliary graph, and solving it exactly recovers a primal potential. Finally, a ground-vertex reduction from regularized objectives to the Poisson solver gives randomized almost-linear-time resolvent/proximal primitives for the same cut-based hypergraph Laplacian.

</details>


### [121] [Online Coloring for Graphs of Large Odd Girth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27690)
*Hirotaka Yoneda,Masataka Yoneda*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: This paper presents a deterministic online coloring algorithm for graphs with large odd girth that achieves O(n^ε) colors for any ε > 0, substantially improving upon Kierstead's 1998 O(n^(1/2)) bound.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Online graph coloring is a fundamental problem in graph theory and algorithm design. The previous best result by Kierstead (1998) achieved O(n^(1/2)) colors for graphs with odd girth at least 7, leaving room for improvement in the color bound.

Method: The paper presents a deterministic online coloring algorithm that exploits properties of graphs with large odd girth to achieve the improved bounds.

Result: The paper provides an algorithm that can color graphs with odd girth at least some sufficiently large g' (depending on ε) using O(n^ε) colors, significantly improving the previous O(n^(1/2)) bound.

Conclusion: This work significantly improves upon Kierstead's O(n^(1/2)) bound by achieving O(n^ε) colors for sufficiently large odd girth, and is the first to achieve this sub-polynomial coloring bound for this problem class.

Abstract: We study the problem of online coloring for graphs with large odd girth. The best previously known algorithm uses $O(n^{1/2})$ colors, which was discovered by Kierstead in 1998. This algorithm works when the odd girth is 7 or more. In this paper, we provide the following: for every $\varepsilon > 0$, there exists a constant $g' \in \{3, 5, 7, \dots\}$ such that graphs with odd girth at least $g'$ can be deterministically colored online using $O(n^{\varepsilon})$ colors.

</details>


### [122] [Simpler and Improved Replacement Path Coverings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27966)
*Davide Bilò,Shiri Chechik,Keerti Choudhary,Sarel Cohen,Martin Schirneck*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本论文简化了$(L,f)$-Replacement Path Covering的去随机化过程，利用条件期望方法避免了复杂的代数编码技术，在$f = o(\log L)$假设下将覆盖值降至$\widetilde{O}(fL^{f+o(1)})$、查询时间降至$\widetilde{O}(f^{5/2}L^{o(1)})$。同时给出了新的随机构造和改进的下界，对于$f = o(\log L)$情形在$L^{o(1)}$项内达到最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: $(L,f)$-Replacement Path Covering (RPC) 是设计容错图数据结构的重要工具，在图论与分布式计算中有广泛应用。前人的确定化方法（Karthik and Parter 2024）虽然实现了去随机化，但使用了涉及纠错码的复杂代数机制，且覆盖值较高。论文旨在简化确定化过程，降低覆盖值并提升查询效率，同时探索RPC的最优覆盖值界限。

Method: 采用条件期望（conditional expectations）方法进行简化的去随机化，并通过代数编码技术的简化避免了原有的纠错码等重型代数工具。新随机构造和下界改进则通过其他组合技术实现。

Result: 2. 新的随机构造和下界改进：对于$f = o(\log L)$，给出$\widetilde{O}((L/f)^f L^{o(1)})$覆盖值的RPC，并在$L^{o(1)}$项内证明其紧致性

Conclusion: 本文通过条件期望方法简化了$(L,f)$-RPC的确定化过程，在降低覆盖值的同时显著提升了查询时间效率。研究还给出了新的随机构造算法和改进的下界，特别对于$f = o(\log L)$情形，给出了$\widetilde{O}((L/f)^f L^{o(1)})$覆盖值的RPC，并证明其在$L^{o(1)}$项内是最优的。

Abstract: An important tool in the design of fault-tolerant graph data structures are $(L,f)$-replacement path coverings (RPCs). An RPC is a family $\mathcal{G}$ of subgraphs of a given graph $G$ such that, for every set $F$ of at most $f$ edges, there is a subfamily $\mathcal{G}_F \,{\subseteq}\, \mathcal{G}$ with the following properties. (1) No subgraph in $\mathcal{G}_F$ contains an edge of $F$. (2) For each pair of vertices $s,t$ that have a shortest path in $G-F$ with at most $L$ edges, one such path also exists in some subgraph in $\mathcal{G}_F$. The covering value of the RPC is the total number $|\mathcal{G}|$ of subgraphs. The query time is the time needed to compute the subfamily $\mathcal{G}_F$ given the set $F$. Weimann and Yuster [TALG'13] devised a randomized RPC with covering value $\widetilde{O}(fL^f)$ and query time $\widetilde{O}(f^2 L^f)$. This was derandomized by Karthik and Parter [TALG'24], who also reduced the query time to $\widetilde{O}(f^2 L)$. Their approach uses some heavy algebraic machinery involving error-correcting codes and an increased covering value of $O((cfL \log n)^{f+1})$ for some constant $c > 1$. We instead devise a much simpler derandomization via conditional expectations that lowers the covering value back to $\widetilde{O}(fL^{f+o(1)})$ and decreases the query time to $\widetilde{O}(f^{5/2}L^{o(1)})$, assuming $f = o(\log L)$. We also investigate the optimal covering value of any $(L,f)$-replacement path covering (deterministic or randomized) for different parameter ranges. We provide a new randomized construction as well as improving a known lower bound, also by Karthik and Parter. For example, for $f = o(\log L)$, we give an RPC with $\widetilde{O}( (L/f)^f L^{o(1)})$ subgraphs and show that this is tight up to the $L^{o(1)}$ term.

</details>


### [123] [Distributed Santa Claus via Global Rounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27983)
*Tijn de Vos,Leo Wennmann,Malte Baumecker,Yannic Maus,Florian Schager*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文首次在分布式CONGEST模型中研究Santa Claus问题，证明O(log n/log log n)近似比的复杂度下界为Θ̃(√n + D)轮，且该下界对任意近似算法都成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Santa Claus问题是一个NP难问题，在顺序模型中已被广泛研究，但在分布式模型中尚未被探索。该问题在资源分配和公平性优化方面具有重要实际意义。

Method: 采用了分布式算法设计与分析技术，在CONGEST模型中设计近似算法，并通过对通信模式的下界分析证明该近似比和复杂度下界。

Result: 证明了在CONGEST模型中，计算O(log n / log log n)近似解的复杂度为Θ̃(√n + D)轮。更重要的是，给出了一个更强的下界Θ̃(√n + D)，该下界对任意近似算法都成立。

Conclusion: 该研究首次在分布式（CONGEST）模型中研究了Santa Claus问题，表明可在O(log n / log log n)近似比内达到O(√n + D)轮的复杂度，并证明该复杂度下界对任意近似算法都成立，这是该领域的一个重要突破。

Abstract: In this paper, we consider the Santa Claus problem in the CONGEST model. This NP-hard problem can be modeled as a bipartite graph of children and gifts where an edge indicates that a child desires a gift. Notably, each gift can have a different value. The goal is to assign the gifts to the children such that the least happy child is as happy as possible. Even though this is a well-studied problem in the sequential setting, we obtain the first results the distributed setting. In particular, we show that the complexity of computing an $\mathcal{O}(\log n/\log \log n)$-approximation is $\hat Θ(\sqrt n+D)$ rounds, where our $\widetildeΩ(\sqrt n+D)$-round lower bound is even stronger and holds for any approximation.

</details>


### [124] [Succinct Graph Representations and Algorithmic Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.28096)
*Ahammed Ullah,Alex Pothen*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 该论文提出利用对偶团覆盖（DCC）表示来挖掘图的稠密局部结构，从而同时改善图算法的时间与空间效率。通过设计succinct DCC表示和相应的representation-aware算法，在连通分量、BFS、DFS、最大匹配等问题上取得了显著的性能提升，实验验证平均内存节省约9倍、总时间加速约6.5倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统图表示（如邻接表）在处理大规模图时面临时间与空间的双重压力，而图的局部稠密结构尚未被充分挖掘利用。作者试图通过利用图中dense local structure来同时改进算法的时间和空间效率。

Method: 定义了DCC（对偶团覆盖）表示框架（C为覆盖边的团集合，L为C的对偶关联关系），识别并构造了succinct DCC表示（紧凑且可在多项式时间内构造），并在此基础上开发了针对基本图问题的representation-aware算法，如连通分量、BFS森林、DFS森林和最大匹配等，时间复杂度与DCC表示大小而非边数成正比。

Result: 提出的算法在时间和空间上均能达到或优于传统标准表示的算法；连通分量算法平均实现约9倍内存节省（最高35倍）和约6.5倍总时间加速（最高35倍）；succinctness特性是DCC表示在算法应用中有效的关键因素。

Conclusion: 通过在真实世界和合成图上的实验验证，DCC表示在内存节省和运行速度上均表现优异。该工作为图算法提供了一种compact且representation-aware的新范式。

Abstract: We propose new graph representations that exploit dense local structure to improve time and space simultaneously. Given an undirected graph $G$, we define a dual clique cover (DCC) representation of $G$ to be the pair $(C, L)$, where $C$ is a collection of cliques that covers the edges of $G$ and $L$ is the incidence dual of $C$. We identify classes of polynomial-time constructible DCC representations that are compact and call them succinct DCC representations. We then develop representation-aware algorithms for several fundamental graph problems.
  We show that graph primitives such as connected components, breadth-first search forests, depth-first search forests, and maximal matchings can be computed in time proportional to the size of a DCC representation rather than the number of edges. Combined with our succinct DCC representations, these results give a class of algorithms that either match or improve the time and space bounds of their counterparts on standard graph representations. Furthermore, we design several algorithms for constructing succinct DCC representations and establish provable guarantees on their efficiency.
  We evaluate several graph algorithms on DCC representations against adjacency-list-based implementations on a large collection of real-world and synthetic graphs. All evaluated applications show substantial execution memory savings and total-time speedups; for example, the connected components algorithm achieves about $9\times$ execution memory savings on average, with a maximum of $35\times$, and about $6.5\times$ total-time speedups on average, with a maximum of $35\times$. We also evaluate several DCC construction algorithms and find that the succinctness property plays a key role in making DCC representations effective for algorithmic applications.

</details>


<div id='cs.CY'></div>

# cs.CY [[Back]](#toc)

### [125] [The Impact of LLM Self-Consistency and Reasoning Effort on Automated Scoring Accuracy and Cost](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26954)
*Scott Frohn*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 研究表明，在LLM自动评分任务中，战略性模型选择和推理努力调优优于模型集成；增加集成规模不能带来显著收益，而提高推理努力可线性提升准确率；成本效益最优方案为GPT-5.4 Nano/Mini配合零推理配置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前自动评分研究中常采用模型集成方法提升准确率，但集成带来的计算成本与实际收益是否匹配尚不明确，需要系统比较集成策略与单模型策略的优劣。

Method: 对比self-consistency（模型内多数投票）与推理努力调节策略；使用高中数学对话式评估数据（900个学生对话）评估OpenAI和Google的前沿及低成本模型；通过效率前沿分析权衡准确率与成本。

Result: 温度采样显著提升准确率；集成规模扩大（1-7个模型）无显著收益；推理努力与评分准确率呈显著正相关但因模型家族而异；Gemini 3.1 Pro Preview低推理配置准确率最高但成本高，GPT-5.4 Nano/Mini无推理配置性价比最优。

Conclusion: Strategic model selection and reasoning effort tuning are more effective than ensembling for LLM-based automated scoring. Gemini 3.1 Pro Preview achieves highest accuracy but at high cost, while GPT-5.4 Nano/Mini offer optimal cost-performance ratio. Temperature sampling should replace deterministic inference for scoring tasks.

Abstract: Strategic model selection and reasoning settings are more effective than ensembling for optimizing automated scoring with large language models (LLMs). We examined self-consistency (intra-model majority voting) and reasoning effort for scoring conversation-based assessment items in high school mathematics, evaluating 900 student conversations against human-scored ground truths using frontier and low-cost models from OpenAI and Google. Temperature sampling significantly improved accuracy over deterministic calls, but increasing ensemble size (j = 1 to 7) produced no significant gains. Higher reasoning effort showed a significant positive linear trend with scoring accuracy, though the benefit varied by model family. An efficiency frontier analysis identified Gemini 3.1 Pro Preview at low reasoning as the most accurate but costly configuration; GPT-5.4 Nano and Mini with no reasoning offered the best cost-performance balance.

</details>


### [126] [Policy-Governed LLM Routing with Intent Matching for Instrument Laboratories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26955)
*Emmanuel A. Olowe,Danial Chitnis*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一个面向工程实验室的 LLM 辅导路由与治理系统，包含 Routiium（多后端网关）和 EduRouter（策略感知路由服务）两个组件，在保持学习挑战性的同时显著降低成本并提升辅导质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工程实验室的 AI 辅导系统面临辅助充分性与学习机会保留之间的张力，现有系统对辅助时机、内容或成本的控制有限，需要一个可治理的 LLM 辅助框架。

Method: 该研究提出了一个包含两个组件的路由与治理系统：1) Routiium——一个兼容 OpenAI 的网关，管理多个 LLM 后端，具有可配置的提示修改和使用日志功能；2) EduRouter——一个策略感知的路由服务，执行按实验室的预算限制、审批工作流和基于嵌入的问答匹配。研究通过两个工程实验室（LED 特性分析、RC 电路分析）的轨迹驱动模拟和 100 查询的实时模型回放进行评估。

Result: 模拟实验中，治理策略（P1/P2）相比无治理操作（P0）将挑战对齐指数从 0.90 提升至 0.98，叠加依从性评分从 0.69 提升至 0.87，productive-struggle 窗口从 1.4 增加至 3.6 个模拟轮次。100 查询回放中，EduRouter 将 75% 的查询路由至本地模型，token 成本降低 66%（$0.087 vs $0.26），同时对 89 意图问答库保持 1.0 的规范命中率。

Conclusion: 研究团队发布了 Routiium、EduRouter、规范任务工具和模拟器配置，以支持复制和未来课堂研究。该系统为工程实验室的 AI 辅导提供了可复用的技术框架和开源工具。

Abstract: AI tutoring systems in engineering labs face a tension between providing sufficient assistance and preserving learning opportunities. Existing systems typically offer instructors limited control over assistance timing, content, or cost. This paper describes a routing and governance system for LLM-based lab assistance comprising two components: Routiium, an OpenAI-compatible gateway that manages multiple LLM backends with configurable prompt modifications and usage logging, and EduRouter, a policy-aware routing service that enforces per-lab budgets, approval workflows, and embedding-based question matching. We evaluated the system using trace-driven simulation calibrated from two engineering labs (LED characterization, RC circuit analysis) and a 100-query replay through live models. In simulations, governed policies (P1/P2) increased challenge-alignment index from 0.90 to 0.98 and overlay-adherence score from 0.69 to 0.87 compared to ungoverned operation (P0). The productive-struggle window metric increased from 1.4 to 3.6 simulated turns before high-scaffold hints appeared. In the 100-query replay, EduRouter routed 75% of queries to a local model, reducing token costs by 66% ($0.087 vs. $0.26 for all-premium routing) while maintaining canonical hit rate of 1.0 for the curated 89-intent question bank. We release Routiium, EduRouter, canonical-task tooling, and simulator configurations to support replication and future classroom studies.

</details>


### [127] [Can AI be a moral victim? The role of moral patiency and ownership perceptions in ethical judgments of using AI-generated content](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26956)
*Hyesun Choung,Soojong Kim*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本研究通过实验揭示了人们对AI生成内容存在道德宽容倾向，复制AI作品相比人类作品受到更轻的道德评判。这种现象的根本原因在于AI被认为缺乏道德痛感，且人类被视为AI生成内容的所有者。拟人化命名会间接影响道德判断标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着生成式AI的广泛应用，关于AI生成内容的著作权和剽窃问题引发伦理争议。现有研究对人们如何道德评判AI生成内容的使用行为及其内在机制尚不清楚，因此需要探讨人类对AI创作内容的伦理判断逻辑。

Method: 实验设计：参与者评估两份实质相似的稿件，原作者被描述为人类、AI系统或具有拟人化名称的AI agent。研究检验了道德判决、剽窃感知和内疚感的差异，并通过中介分析探究道德宽容的机制。

Result: 复制AI生成内容相比复制人类作品被视为更少的剽窃行为，引发更少的内疚感。中介分析表明，这种道德宽容源于两个机制：（1）AI的道德痛感较低（即感知AI遭受伤害的能力较弱）；（2）将AI生成内容的使用权更多归于人类用户。拟人化线索通过降低所有权感知间接影响道德评估。

Conclusion: 本研究表明，人们在使用AI生成内容时存在道德疏离现象，这与AI缺乏道德痛感（moral patiency）以及所有权归属的人性化感知有关。拟人化线索会通过降低所有权感知间接影响道德判断。这一发现揭示了人类对AI创作内容的伦理评价标准与人类作品存在显著差异，并提示在AI生成内容广泛应用的背景下，现有的学术诚信和所有权概念可能需要重新审视。

关键发现：
1. 复制AI生成内容受到更宽容的道德评判
2. AI缺乏道德痛感是宽容态度的重要来源
3. 拟人化命名会间接影响道德评估
4. 人类使用AI工具时被视为内容所有者

Abstract: The growing use of generative AI raises ethical concerns about authorship and plagiarism. This study examines how people judge the reuse of AI-generated content, focusing on moral patiency and ownership perceptions. In an experiment, participants evaluated two substantively similar manuscripts in which the original source was described as authored by a human, an AI system, or an AI agent with a human-like name. Results showed that copying AI-generated work was judged less unethical, less plagiaristic, and less guilt-inducing than copying human-authored work. Mediation analyses revealed that this leniency stemmed from lower perceptions of AI's capacity to suffer harm (moral patiency) and greater ownership attributed to the human writer reusing AI-generated content. Anthropomorphic cues shaped moral evaluations indirectly by reducing perceived ownership. These findings shed light on how people morally disengage when using AI-generated work and highlight differences in how ethical judgments are applied to human versus AI-created content.

</details>


### [128] [Simulating Validity: Modal Decoupling in MLLM Generated Feedback on Science Drawings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26957)
*Arne Bewersdorff,Nejla Yuruk,Xiaoming Zhai*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: This study evaluates GPT-5.1's ability to provide feedback on middle school students' hand-drawn scientific models. Finding that 41.3% of feedback contains grounding errors (modal decoupling), where feedback contradicts or ignores visual evidence in the drawings. While prompting strategies help reduce errors, they cannot eliminate them, indicating fundamental limitations requiring new grounding mechanisms for valid educational feedback.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLLMs are increasingly used to generate feedback on students' hand-drawn scientific models, but the validity of such feedback depends on whether model claims are grounded in the specific visual evidence of the student drawing. There's a risk of "modal decoupling" where outputs appear pedagogically plausible while contradicting the drawing or treating depicted elements as missing.

Method: N=150 middle school drawings from kinetic molecular theory unit spanning 5 modeling tasks and 3 competence levels generated N=300 feedback instances with GPT-5.1. Outputs were coded for four grounding error types: object mismatch, attribute mismatch, relation mismatch, and false absence. An inventory-list-first workflow was also tested to reduce errors.

Result: Grounding failures were common: 41.3% of feedback instances contained at least one error. The inventory-list-first workflow reduced several error categories and lowered the overall error rate, but approximately one in three outputs remained flawed. False absence was the dominant failure mode. Feedback that appears visually grounded offered little diagnostic value for identifying invalid instances.

Conclusion: Modal decoupling is a substantial limitation in MLLM-generated feedback that cannot be resolved through common prompting strategies. Even with improved workflows, approximately one in three outputs remain flawed. Valid educational feedback will require grounding mechanisms beyond current approaches.

Abstract: In science education, students frequently construct hand-drawn visual models of scientific phenomena. These drawings rely on a visual structure where information is encoded through visual objects, their attributes, and relationships. Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) are increasingly used to generate feedback on students' hand-drawn scientific models. However, the validity of such feedback depends on whether model claims are grounded in the specific visual evidence of the student drawing. This study uncovers grounding failures, consistent with modal decoupling, in off-the-shelf MLLM feedback, where outputs remain pedagogically plausible in form while contradicting the drawing or treating depicted elements as missing. Using N = 150 middle school drawings from a kinetic molecular theory unit spanning five modeling tasks and three competence levels, we generated N = 300 feedback instances with GPT-5.1. All outputs were coded for four grounding error types: object mismatch, attribute mismatch, relation mismatch, and false absence. Grounding failures were common: 41.3% of feedback instances contained at least one error. An inventory-list-first workflow reduced several error categories and lowered the overall error rate, but it did not resolve the underlying limitation: approximately one in three outputs remained flawed, with false absence as the dominant failure mode. Moreover, feedback that appears visually grounded offered little diagnostic value for identifying invalid instances. The findings indicate that modal decoupling is a substantial limitation and that valid feedback will require grounding mechanisms beyond common prompting strategies.

</details>


### [129] [Designing Ethical Learning for Agentic AI: Toegye Yi Hwang's Ethical Emotion Regulation Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26958)
*Ji Yeon Kim*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向智能体AI学习设计的伦理情感调节框架，灵感源自韩国儒学家退溪的道德-情感哲学，将伦理情感反馈系统重构为与智能体周期对齐的五阶段架构，配套开发了EEFS评估工具以实现智能体AI系统道德-情感对齐的系统性评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前框架仅将情感视为被动反馈或参与度优化机制，未能覆盖自主决策循环中的规范性调节需求。智能体AI系统需要超越传统的反应性情感模型，建立能够支持道德-情感过程持续调节的设计范式。

Method: 基于韩国儒学家退溪（Toegye Yi Hwang）的道德-情感哲学，将道德情感理论重构为与智能体周期对齐的五阶段架构（EEFS），并为每个阶段制定具体设计原则和场景分类，同时开发配套的评估工具。

Result: 提出了一个基于退溪哲学的道德-情感调节框架，将EEFS重构为与智能体周期对齐的五阶段架构，并配套开发了EEFS评估工具以支持系统性评估。

Conclusion: 该研究为智能体AI学习设计中的道德-情感调节提供了系统性评估工具，弥合了传统情感理论与自主决策循环之间的鸿沟，为可解释且合乎伦理的智能体AI系统提供了设计规范。五个阶段分别对应感知、评估、决策、实施与反馈环节，每个阶段均有明确的设计原则和场景分类。研究还开发了EEFS评估工具以实现对智能体AI系统道德-情感对齐的系统性测评。

Abstract: Agentic AI systems capable of autonomous goal setting and proactive intervention introduce new challenges for regulating moral-emotional processes in learning environments. Existing frameworks typically treat emotion as reactive feedback or engagement optimization, overlooking the need for normative regulation across autonomous decision cycles.This paper proposes an ethical emotion regulation framework for agentic AI learning design inspired by Toegye Yi Hwang's moral-emotional philosophy. The Ethical Emotion Feedback System (EEFS) is reconstructed as a five-stage architecture aligned with agentic cycles, articulating stage-specific design principles and scenario classifications.An EEFS Evaluation Instrument is introduced to enable systematic assessment of moral-emotional alignment in agentic AI systems.

</details>


### [130] [DeepTutor: Towards Agentic Personalized Tutoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26962)
*Bingxi Zhao,Jiahao Zhang,Xubin Ren,Zirui Guo,Tianzhe Chu,Yi Ma,Chao Huang*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: DeepTutor是一个面向个性化辅导的智能原生开源框架，通过混合个性化引擎和动态多分辨率记忆实现学习者画像的持续演化，并构建双向闭环辅导循环实现引用溯源解题与难度校准问题的生成。框架包含TutorBot主动式多智能体层和TutorBench评估基准，在保持智能体推理能力的同时显著提升了个性化辅导质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM教育应用存在两大缺陷：传统辅导系统依赖静态预训练知识无法适应个体学习者；现有RAG增强系统缺乏个性化引导反馈能力。

Method: 提出混合个性化引擎（静态知识基础+动态多分辨率记忆）、构建双向闭环辅导循环（引用溯源解题+难度校准问题生成）、开发TutorBot主动式多智能体层（可扩展技能+多渠道统一接入）、构建TutorBench评估基准（以学习者为中心的源溯源档案+第一人称交互协议）。

Result: DeepTutor在提升个性化辅导质量的同时保持了一般智能体推理能力，已通过五个权威基准测试验证。

Conclusion: DeepTutor为下一代AI个性化辅导系统提供了独特见解，是一个具有开源性质的智能辅导框架。

Abstract: Education represents one of the most promising real-world applications for Large Language Models (LLMs). However, conventional tutoring systems rely on static pre-training knowledge that lacks adaptation to individual learners, while existing RAG-augmented systems fall short in delivering personalized, guided feedback. To bridge this gap, we present DeepTutor, an agent-native open-source framework for personalized tutoring where every feature shares a common personalization substrate. We propose a hybrid personalization engine that couples static knowledge grounding with dynamic multi-resolution memory, distilling interaction history into a continuously evolving learner profile. Moreover, we construct a closed tutoring loop that bidirectionally couples citation-grounded problem solving with difficulty-calibrated question generation. The personalization substrate further supports collaborative writing, multi-agent deep research, and interactive guided learning, enabling cross-modality coherence. To move beyond reactive interfaces, we introduce TutorBot, a proactive multi-agent layer that deploys tutoring capabilities through extensible skills and unified multi-channel access, providing consistent experience across platforms. To better evaluate such tutoring systems, we construct TutorBench, a student-centric benchmark with source-grounded learner profiles and a first-person interactive protocol that measures adaptive tutoring from the learner's perspective. We further evaluate foundational agentic reasoning abilities across five authoritative benchmarks. Experiments show that DeepTutor improves personalized tutoring quality while maintaining general agentic reasoning abilities. We hope DeepTutor provides unique insights into next-generation AI-powered and personalized tutoring systems for the community.

</details>


### [131] [A Discipline-Agnostic AI Literacy Course for Academic Research: Architecture, Pedagogy, and Implementation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27225)
*Gideon K. Gogovi*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本研究介绍了一门在利哈伊大学开设的AI辅助研究入门课程，旨在填补现有AI素养教育的空白。课程围绕AI辅助文献综述的四个认知阶段组织，包含验证规范和AI归属实践。实证数据显示学生在关键能力上取得显著进步，可作为AI研究素养教育的可复制模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI素养教育存在两极分化的问题：技术导向的AI开发课程受众狭窄，而通用素养培训过于简短，无法培养学生进行严谨研究所需的持续性、实践性能力。特别是AI辅助文献综述这一核心研究能力的系统性培训存在明显空白。

Method: 本研究采用课程开发与实证评估相结合的方法。课程架构设计为四个递进式模块，对应文献综述任务的认知需求：论文理解、知识分类体系构建、研究空白识别、文献综述合成与产出。每个模块嵌入明确的验证规范和标准化的AI归属实践。采用无先修要求、跨学科的设计，通过前后测问卷收集学生自信度变化数据。

Result: 前后测数据显示学生自信度显著提升，效果量最大的是AI归属实践（d=+2.40），其次是幻觉检测（d=+1.45）和负责任AI使用（d=+1.33），与课程设计重点高度吻合，验证了教学策略的有效性。

Conclusion: 该课程为AI研究素养教育提供了一个可复制的模式，可供其他高等教育机构借鉴和推广。

Abstract: The rapid integration of generative AI into academic workflows demands curricula that equip students not only with tool proficiency but with the critical judgment to use those tools responsibly in scholarly work. Existing offerings cluster around two inadequate poles: technical AI development courses serving narrow specialist audiences, and brief general-literacy interventions that cannot develop the sustained, practice-based competencies rigorous research requires. This paper reports the design, theoretical rationale, and implementation of BSTA 495/395: Getting Started with AI-Assisted Research, developed and delivered at Lehigh University (Spring 2026). The course addresses an underserved gap: the competencies required for rigorous AI-assisted literature review. Its architecture organizes instruction into four sequential modules aligned with the cognitive demands of that task: comprehension of individual papers, construction and validation of knowledge taxonomies, identification of research gaps, and synthesis and production of complete literature reviews. Each module embeds an explicit verification discipline and standardized AI attribution practice. Prerequisite-free and discipline-agnostic, the course enrolls upper-level undergraduates and graduate students across all fields with differentiated assessment expectations. Pre- and post-course survey data from the inaugural offering indicate substantial self-reported confidence gains, with the largest in hallucination detection (d = +1.45), responsible AI use (d = +1.33), and AI attribution practice (d = +2.40), consistent with the course's design emphasis. The course constitutes a replicable model for the emerging genre of AI research literacy curricula.

</details>


### [132] [Addressing the Reality Gap: A Three-Tension Framework for Agentic AI Adoption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27245)
*Jason Fournier,Kacper Łodzikowski*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 该章节探讨了教育领域AI应用面临的三重张力——实施可行性、适应速度和使命一致性，并提出相应框架以指导K-12和高等教育的AI决策，旨在帮助教育领导者把握生成式和Agentic AI带来的机遇，同时应对其带来的挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI已快速进入教育领域，但学校和大学的应对能力滞后。更重要的是，新一代更具自主性的Agentic AI系统即将到来，既带来个性化教育的机遇，也带来更大 disruption 的风险。

Method: 首先回顾生成式AI和Agentic AI在各行业及教育一线应用的早期证据，然后提出三张力框架来指导K-12和高等教育的AI决策与设计。

Result: 提出三张力分析框架（实施可行性、适应速度、使命一致性），并展示了如何应用该框架进行负责任的AI部署规划，同时识别了课程链接型AI Agent和教育者参与AI设计等新兴趋势。

Conclusion: 教育领导者应主动把握AI机遇与挑战，确保技术服务于教学与学习的提升。

Abstract: Generative AI has rapidly entered education through free consumer tools, outpacing the ability of schools and universities to respond. Now a new wave of more autonomous agentic AI systems--with the capacity to plan and act towards goals--promises both greater educational personalization and greater disruption. This chapter argues that successfully navigating these innovations requires balancing three core tensions: (1) Implementation Feasibility, or the practical capacity to integrate AI sustainably into real classrooms; (2) Adaptation Speed, or the mismatch between fast-evolving AI capabilities and the slower pace of educational change; and (3) Mission Alignment, or the need to ensure AI applications uphold educational values such as equity, privacy, and pedagogical integrity. First, we review early evidence of generative and agentic AI in various sectors and in frontline education to illustrate these tensions in context. Then, we present a three-tension framework to guide decision-makers in evaluating and designing AI initiatives across K-12 and higher education. We provide examples of how the framework can be applied to plan responsible AI deployments, and we identify emerging trends--such as curriculum-linked AI agents and educator-informed AI design--along with open research directions. We conclude the chapter with recommendations for educational leaders to proactively engage with the opportunities and challenges of AI, so that this technology can be harnessed to enhance teaching and learning in the decade ahead.

</details>


### [133] [The Likelihood Ratio Wall: Structural Limits on Accurate Risk Assessment for Rare Violence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27282)
*Marco Pollanen*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 研究发现预审风险评估工具在预测稀有暴力事件时存在不可逾越的统计限制。即使工具性能指标看起来不错，在低发生率条件下被标记为"高风险"的人仍可能大多数是误判。由于似然比墙的存在和过度执法导致的监控天花板效应，当前数据体系下的特征可能根本无法支持高置信度的个体化羁押决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 每年有超过一百万美国被告接受审前风险评估，用于预测暴力再犯，但这些工具在预测稀有暴力事件（2-5%）时面临基本统计障碍。

Method: 1. 推导出"似然比墙"（Likelihood Ratio Wall）——一个通用的精确度上限；2. 展示后验分数重校准无法解决问题，因为它不能改善工具区分真阳性与假阳性的基础能力；3. 证明"监控天花板"效应——当过度执法在不会再犯的人群中人为膨胀记录的"风险因素"时，即使在相等的犯罪率下，过度执法群体的最大可达精度在结构上更低；4. 将结果转化为"需要羁押人数"（Number Needed to Detain）指标。

Result: 1. 即使暴力再犯率很低（2-5%），要达到50%的阳性预测值（PPV）也需要比当前工具更具有区分能力的工具；2. 工具可能呈现看起来不错的性能指标，但在标记"高暴力风险"时仍可能大部分时候是错误的；3. 后验分数重校准无法改善工具的底层分离能力；4. 过度执法为过度执法群体创造了结构性的精度上限；5. 提出"需要羁押人数"指标来明确传达这种不确定性。

Conclusion: 在当前数据体系下，用于稀有暴力事件的预审风险评估工具可能无法支持高置信度的个体化羁押决定。仅仅讨论公平性指标是不够的，因为特征本身可能不足以支撑高精度的风险判断。

Abstract: Pretrial risk assessment tools are used on over one million U.S. defendants each year, yet their use for predicting rare violent re-offense faces a basic statistical barrier. We derive a universal precision bound -- the Likelihood Ratio Wall -- showing that when violent re-arrest rates are low (2-5%), achieving even a 50% hit rate among people labeled "high risk" (positive predictive value, or PPV) would require tools far more discriminative than current instruments appear to be. For rare outcomes, a tool can have respectable-looking performance metrics and still be wrong most of the time it flags someone as "high risk for violence." We show that post-hoc score recalibration cannot solve this problem because it does not improve the tool's underlying ability to separate true positives from false positives. We further prove a Surveillance Ceiling: when over-policing inflates recorded "risk factors" among those who would not re-offend, the maximum achievable precision is structurally lower for over-policed groups, even at equal offense rates. We translate these results into the Number Needed to Detain (how many people must be detained to prevent one violent offense), and propose that risk reports should communicate this uncertainty explicitly. Our findings suggest that for rare violent outcomes, debates about fairness metrics alone are incomplete: under current data regimes, the available features may not support high-confidence individualized detention decisions.

</details>


### [134] [Bibliometric Mapping of AI-Supported Social Presence in Online Learning Environments: Trends, Collaboration, and Thematic Directions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27344)
*Almer B. Gamboa,Erika M. Pineda,Rhiziel P. Manalese,Aileen P. De Leon,Vernon Grace M. Maniago,Jan Henry B. Sunga,Agnes R. Regala,Roque Francis B. Dianelo,John Paul P. Miranda*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 该研究通过文献计量方法分析了在线学习环境中AI支持的社交存在感研究的发展、影响和协作模式，发现该领域近年来增长迅速，但国际合作不足，伦理问题亟待深入探索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需要系统梳理和理解AI支持的社交存在感研究在在线学习环境中的发展轨迹、影响范围和协作模式，以把握该领域的研究现状与未来方向。

Method: 使用Scopus中的59项开放获取实证研究，应用引用分析、作者合作映射、机构分析和关键词聚类，并采用基于Python的文献计量工具进行分析。

Result: 发表量自2020年起呈上升趋势；研究主要聚焦于参与度、AI工具、教学设计和伦理问题；美国和巴西是领先贡献国；国际合作有限；信任和公平相关的伦理问题正在兴起但尚未被充分探索。

Conclusion: 研究强调在教育中整合伦理考量、跨学科协作以及以学习者为中心的AI应用的重要性。美国和巴西是主要贡献国家，但国际合作仍然有限。信任和公平相关的伦理问题虽已出现但尚未得到充分探索。

Abstract: This study examines the development, influence, and collaboration patterns in AI-supported social presence research within online learning environments. Utilizing 59 open-access empirical studies from Scopus, the study applies citation analysis, co-authorship mapping, institutional analysis, and keyword clustering using Python-based bibliometric tools. Findings reveal an upward trend in publications since 2020, with research focusing on engagement, AI tools, instructional design, and ethical issues. While countries such as the United States and Brazil are leading contributors, international collaboration remains limited. Ethical concerns related to trust and fairness are emerging but underexplored. The study highlights the importance of ethical integration, interdisciplinary collaboration, and learner-centered AI applications in education.

</details>


### [135] [Profiles of AI Dependency: A Latent Class Analysis of Filipino Students' Academic Competencies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27349)
*Emerson Q. Fernando,Julius Ceazar G. Tolentino,Maria Anna D. Cruz,Jordan L. Salenga,Vernon Grace M. Maniago,Juvy C. Grume,Erika M. Pineda,Aileen P. De Leon,John Paul P. Miranda*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本研究调查了651名菲律宾大学生的AI依赖程度及其对学术能力的影响。研究发现学生在研究和写作任务上存在中高度AI依赖，并通过潜在类别分析识别出四类学生群体：独立学习者、选择性AI用户、中度AI用户和AI依赖型学习者。AI依赖型学习者学术能力最弱，表明过度依赖AI可能损害批判性思维和写作等核心技能。研究呼吁教育政策在推广AI素养的同时保护基础学术能力，并建议进一步探索干预策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着菲律宾大学生对人工智能（AI）的依赖日益增加，存在基础学术能力可能下降的担忧。现有研究对这种依赖程度及其对学生核心学术能力的实际影响尚缺乏深入了解。

Method: 采用横断面研究设计，通过问卷调查收集数据，并使用潜在类别分析（LCA）识别AI依赖模式。研究样本为651名来自菲律宾邦板牙省高等教育机构的学生。

Result: 学生表现出中度到高度的AI依赖，尤其在研究和写作任务方面。LCA识别出四种不同的学生画像：高度参与的独立学习者、选择性AI用户、中度AI用户和AI依赖型学习者。其中，AI依赖型学习者的学术能力最弱。研究强调了制定融合AI素养的教育政策的必要性，同时需保护基础学术技能。

Conclusion: 该研究建议制定教育政策以平衡AI素养培养与基础学术技能保护。高等教育机构需在技术进步与课程调整之间寻求平衡，以促进批判性思维发展和AI伦理使用。未来研究应探索AI依赖对学术技能影响的纵向效应及干预策略。

Abstract: The increasing dependency among Filipino college students on artificial intelligence (AI) poses concerns about the potential decline of fundamental academic competencies. This study examines the extent of AI dependency and its perceived effects on students' critical thinking, writing skills, learning independence, research skills, and academic engagement. Using a cross-sectional research design, data was collected from 651 students enrolled in higher education institutions (HEIs) in Pampanga, Philippines accredited by the Commission on Higher Education. The survey data was analyzed using Latent Class Analysis (LCA) to identify AI dependency patterns. Findings indicated that students show moderate to high AI dependency, specifically in research and writing tasks. LCA identified four distinct profiles: highly engaged independent learners, selective AI users, moderate AI users, and AI-dependent learners. Notably, AI-dependent learners demonstrated the weakest academic competencies, with significant dependency on AI-generated outputs. The study highlights the need to foster educational policies that integrate AI literacy while preserving essential academic skills. HEIs must also balance technological advancements with curriculum adaptations to promote critical thinking and ethical use of AI. Future research may explore the longitudinal impacts and intervention strategies to mitigate academic skill erosion caused by AI dependency.

</details>


### [136] [Multi-element Persuasion in Social Media Health Communication: Synergistic and Trade-off Effects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27350)
*Weifeng Zhang,Jipeng Tan,Mengye Yang,Yong Min*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本研究通过分析180万条微博帖子，采用聚类分析识别出健康信息的四种主要元素组合模式，提出"核心-边缘-环境"框架来解释多元素组合如何共同影响传播效果，揭示了说服元素之间的协同与权衡关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注单一元素或简单的两两交互作用，对多个元素在真实数字环境中的协同运作缺乏足够洞察。健康社交媒体信息通常由来源线索、诉求、框架和证据等多元素组合构成，但这些元素如何共同作用产生说服效果尚不清晰。

Method: 采用系统视角，基于180万条健康相关的微博帖子进行聚类分析。首先识别反复出现的元素组合，然后评估这些组合与传播效果之间的关系。研究方法结合了计算方法和传播效果测量。

Result: 研究发现：(1)识别出四种反复出现的元素组合模式：机构权威型、叙事型、主张型、语境表达型，这些组合围绕两个核心元素组织成核心结构；(2)更强的传播效果不仅依赖核心结构，还需要与核心结构对齐的边缘元素，二至四个边缘元素的组合通常表现更优；(3)边缘复杂性的最优水平随来源影响力而变化，表明环境因素调节着信息组合与传播效果之间的关系。

Conclusion: 研究提出了"核心-边缘-环境"框架，解释多元素组合如何在社交媒体上产生具有说服力的传播效果。该框架将传播效果理解为多个说服元素之间的协同与权衡结果，并将环境因素纳入分析。这一研究从孤立元素扩展到系统组合视角，为健康传播实践提供了具体建议，同时也为数字传播研究提供了新的理论视角。

Abstract: Health messages on social media are typically constructed through combinations of source cues, appeals, frames, and evidence, which jointly shape communication and persuasive effects. However, prior research has largely focused on single elements or simple pairwise interactions, offering insufficient insight into how multiple elements operate together in real-world digital environments. To address this gap, this study adopts a systems perspective to examine multi-element message combinations. Using 1.8 million health-related Weibo posts, we apply clustering analysis to identify recurring combinations and assess their relationships with communication effects. First, four recurring element combinations are identified: Institutional Authority, Narrative, Assertive Appeal, and Contextual Expression. These combinations function as core structures organized around two key elements. Second, stronger communication effects depend not only on core structures but also on peripheral elements aligned with these structures, with combinations of two to four peripheral elements generally showing greater advantages. Third, the optimal level of peripheral complexity varies with source influence, indicating that environmental factors condition the relationship between message combinations and communication effects. These findings show that communication and persuasive effects are shaped by synergies and trade-offs among multiple persuasive elements. Based on this, the study proposes a Core-Periphery-Environment framework to explain how message combinations generate communication effects with persuasive implications on social media. The study extends research from isolated elements to systems combinations and offers practical implications for health communication.

</details>


### [137] [Structural Dissolution: How Artificial Intelligence Dismantles Coordination Architecture and Reconfigures the Political Economy of Production](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27435)
*Chao Li,Chunyi Zhao*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出"结构溶解框架"，认为AI通过将人类多模态接口（语言、视觉、行为数据）内部化为计算系统，溶解了企业、市场、专家与消费者之间的传统边界。这一过程定义了"接口内部化"机制，使原本的跨主体协调转变为系统内计算。框架挑战了科斯的交易成本理论，认为AI使传统组织边界在经济上变得过时，企业虽存续为法律实体，但作为协调功能载体的角色将被区域数据主权实体所取代。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The paper aims to explain the fundamental restructuring effect of AI on traditional industry coordination architecture. The authors argue that existing Coasian theory (transaction cost minimization determining organizational boundaries) is insufficient to explain what they see as a qualitative transformation of production relations, not merely efficiency gains.

Method: Proposed a new theoretical framework called "Structural Dissolution Framework" that defines the mechanism as "Interface Internalization" - where inter-agent coordination is absorbed into intra-system computation. Used resource-dependent regional economies (seasonal industries) as illustrative case study.

Result: The framework identifies four major structural shifts: (1) erosion of firm and industry boundaries; (2) value creation shifting from physical resources/human collaboration to continuous token flows via data refinement loops; (3) domain-specific data refinement infrastructure becoming the new basis of positional control; (4) emergence of regional data sovereignty entities replacing the coordinating role of firms and markets.

Conclusion: AI renders traditional firm and market boundaries economically obsolete; firms persist as legal entities but lose their coordinating function, becoming mere data nodes within regionally governed AI infrastructure; seasonal industries can transform into continuous economic infrastructure while replacing traditional employment structures.

Abstract: This paper introduces the Structural Dissolution Framework to explain how artificial intelligence restructures the coordination architecture of traditional industries. We argue that AI dissolves the boundaries that once separated firms, markets, experts, and consumers by internalizing human multimodal interfaces, including language, vision, and behavioral data, into computational systems. This process is not merely an efficiency gain but a qualitative transformation of production relations. It generates four major shifts: the erosion of firm and industry boundaries; the movement of value creation from physical resources and human collaboration to continuous token flows produced through data refinement loops; the rise of domain-specific data refinement infrastructure as the new basis of positional control; and the emergence of regional data sovereignty entities as organizational forms that replace the coordinating role of firms and markets. We define this mechanism as Interface Internalization, through which inter-agent coordination is absorbed into intra-system computation. The framework challenges the Coasian view that organizational boundaries are determined by transaction cost minimization, arguing instead that AI makes such boundaries economically obsolete. Firms may continue to exist as legal and physical entities, but their coordinating function is displaced as they become data nodes within regionally governed AI infrastructure. Using resource-dependent regional economies as an illustrative case, the paper shows how AI adoption can both transform seasonal industries into continuous economic infrastructure and replace intermediate coordination roles and traditional employment structures.

</details>


### [138] [Empire Amplifier: Uncovering and Contesting the Prioritization of Colonial Content on Platforms Through Community-Informed Algorithmic Auditing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27498)
*Nel Escher,Bakyt Yrysov,Ashley McDermott,Daniel Chechelnitsky,Hermela Berehan Benyam,Nikola Banovic*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: This paper investigates whether online platforms sustain or endanger Indigenous cultural practice by focusing on Kyrgyz communities' concerns about Russian linguistic influence on YouTube. Through a community-informed algorithmic audit, researchers found that YouTube primarily recommends non-Kyrgyz content to Kyrgyz children, validating caregiver concerns. The study reveals platforms can amplify colonial influence and offers strategies like cross-generational device sharing to align platform behavior with Indigenous values.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kyrgyz communities express concerns that online platforms are expanding Russia's linguistic influence and threatening their heritage language. Caregivers fear YouTube encourages children to speak Russian instead of Kyrgyz. The paper addresses whether platforms sustain or endanger Indigenous cultural practice.

Method: Community-informed algorithmic audit combining ethnographic research and quantitative measurement of YouTube recommendation patterns. The audit investigated how YouTube prioritizes content across Indigenous (Kyrgyz) and non-Indigenous (Russian) languages for child users, conducted in conversation with the Kyrgyz community.

Result: YouTube primarily recommends non-Kyrgyz content to Kyrgyz children, even when children signal clear preferences for Kyrgyz content. Platform recommendations reinforce Kyrgyz children's offline uptake of colonial language ideologies.

Conclusion: Platforms can amplify colonial influence rather than revitalizing Indigenous cultural heritage. Algorithmic systems may reimpose oppressive power structures that decolonial efforts have sought to dismantle. The paper offers actionable strategies (e.g., cross-generational device sharing) to reduce colonial language recommendations.

Abstract: Though online platforms claim to amplify Indigenous voices, Indigenous communities are worried that these systems are instead eroding their language and culture. We conduct a community-informed algorithmic audit to explore whether online platforms sustain or endanger Indigenous cultural practice. First, we review ethnographic research pertaining to the cultural anxieties of a specific Indigenous community, as Indigenous peoples are not a monolith. We consider concerns from Kyrgyz communities who believe that platforms are expanding Russia's linguistic influence and threatening their language. Next, we construct and conduct an algorithmic audit in conversation with the community. Our audit investigates deep-seated fears among Kyrgyz caregivers that YouTube encourages their children to speak Russian instead of Kyrgyz, their heritage language. We measure how the YouTube recommendation algorithm prioritizes content across Indigenous and non-Indigenous languages for child users. Our results validate caregiver concerns, as we find that YouTube primarily recommends non-Kyrgyz content to Kyrgyz children, even when children signal clear preferences for Kyrgyz content. Thus, platform recommendations reinforce Kyrgyz children's offline uptake of colonial language ideologies. Finally, we evaluate strategies to align platform behavior with Indigenous values. We identify effective end-user practices for reducing the proportion of Russian-language YouTube recommendations, like cross-generational device sharing. Overall, our work uncovers how platforms can amplify colonial influence, rather than revitalizing Indigenous cultural heritage. We encourage researchers to consider how algorithmic systems can reimpose oppressive power structures that decolonial efforts have sought to dismantle.

</details>


### [139] [Towards an Ethical AI Curriculum: A Pan-African, Culturally Contextualized Framework for Primary and Secondary Education](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27708)
*Abidemi Kuburat Adedeji,Franklin Tchakounte,Sulaiman Oluwasegun Yusuff*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: This paper proposes a Pan-African framework for integrating AI education into African primary and secondary schools, addressing the imperative to prepare Africa's youth for AI-mediated labor markets while avoiding uncritical import of external curricula. The framework synthesizes continental/national policy documents, decolonial pedagogy, and Ubuntu-grounded AI governance, contributing a structured system of six guiding principles, four curriculum domains, five ethical competencies, and age-banded progression, with planned empirical validation across multilingual African contexts.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: To prepare Africa's young population (over 60% under 25) for AI-mediated labor markets without uncritically importing curricula from other linguistic, cultural, and socio-political contexts. The African Union's Continental AI Strategy (2024) and the 2025 Africa Declaration on AI have elevated these questions to the continental agenda.

Method: Structured conceptual synthesis of continental and national policy documents, peer-reviewed scholarship on AI ethics, AI literacy, decolonial pedagogy, and Ubuntu-grounded AI governance. Planned empirical validation includes Delphi study, teacher surveys across multiple linguistic contexts (anglophone, francophone, lusophone, and arabophone), and multi-country classroom piloting.

Result: A proposed Pan-African, culturally contextualized, and ethically grounded framework comprising: (i) six guiding principles, four curriculum domains, five ethical competencies, and age-banded progression from lower primary to upper secondary; (ii) comparative analysis of continental and national policy contexts; (iii) explicit mapping between global AI-ethics principles and Ubuntu-informed relational ethics; (iv) planned empirical validation programme; (v) targeted recommendations for policymakers, educators, civil society, and international partners.

Conclusion: An ethical AI curriculum can serve as a transformative tool for equity, innovation, and social justice, with a proposed research agenda to embed ethics, resilience, and critical thinking at the core of Africa's digital future. The framework offers actionable guidance for policymakers, educators, civil society, and international partners.

Abstract: Artificial intelligence (AI) is now embedded in educational, civic, and economic systems worldwide. For African primary and secondary education, this creates a double imperative: to prepare a young population (over sixty per cent of Africans are under twenty-five) for AI-mediated labour markets without uncritically importing curricula designed for other linguistic, cultural, and socio-political contexts. The African Union's Continental AI Strategy (2024) and the 2025 Africa Declaration on AI have elevated these questions to the continental agenda. This paper proposes a Pan-African, culturally contextualised, and ethically grounded framework for integrating AI education into African primary and secondary schools. The paper is a structured conceptual synthesis of continental and national policy documents, peer-reviewed scholarship on AI ethics, AI literacy, decolonial pedagogy, and Ubuntu-grounded AI governance. We contribute: (i) a framework of six guiding principles, four curriculum domains, five ethical competencies, and an age-banded progression from lower primary to upper secondary; (ii) a comparative analysis of continental and national policy contexts; (iii) an explicit mapping between global AI-ethics principles and Ubuntu-informed relational ethics; (iv) a planned empirical validation programme combining a Delphi study, teacher surveys across anglophone, francophone, lusophone, and arabophone contexts, and multi-country classroom piloting; and (v) targeted recommendations for policymakers, educators, civil society, and international partners. We argue that an ethical AI curriculum can serve as a transformative tool for equity, innovation, and social justice, and outline a research agenda to embed ethics, resilience, and critical thinking at the core of Africa's digital future.

</details>


### [140] [To Build or Not to Build? Factors that Lead to Non-Development or Abandonment of AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.28053)
*Shreya Chappidi,Jatinder Singh*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本研究调查AI系统放弃的驱动因素，通过文献综述和实证分析开发了六类因素分类法，发现学术界强调的伦理风险在真实案例中只是驱动因素的一部分，组织放弃AI开发的动机多样且常与伦理无关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前负责任AI研究主要关注已部署系统，但缺乏对开发前决策（是否构建AI系统）的关注。早期决策决定最终发布哪些系统，是潜在的未被充分探索的干预点。

Method: 范围性综述（学术文献、民间社会资源、灰色文献）+ 主题分析 + AI事件数据库和从业者调查的实证验证

Result: 开发了六类别分类法（伦理、反馈、生命周期挑战、组织动态、资源约束、法律监管）；实证发现学术强调的伦理风险只是现实案例的一部分，真实驱动因素多样且常与伦理无关。

Conclusion: 识别负责任AI研究的差距和机会：(1) 关注影响组织放弃AI开发的多样化杠杆，(2) 更好地支持对AI系统开发的适当介入或退出决策

Abstract: Responsible AI research typically focuses on examining the use and impacts of deployed AI systems.
